  Three corrections to the brief, each sourced in the file:
  1. The Cs–Rb fine-structure-constant disagreement is 5.5σ (Fan et al. 2023 text; Morel et al. 2020: "more than 5 standard deviations"), not 2.4σ. The ~2.5σ figure is Parker 2018 vs the 2008-g-2 SM prediction. Fan 2023 (g/2 = 1.001 159 652 180 59(13), 0.13 ppt) differs 2.1σ from the Rb-based and 3.9σ from the Cs-based SM prediction; α⁻¹(a_e+SM) = 137.035 999 166(15).
  2. Gurung et al. 2020's "not in agreement" result (18501.02 ± 0.61 vs theory 18498.25 ± 0.08 MHz; 4.5σ is derived, the paper states no σ) is the positronium 2³S₁→2³P₀ (18.5 GHz) interval, not 2³P₂. The 2³S₁→2³P₂ line was re-measured in 2023 (8627.94 ± 0.95 MHz, 1.3σ from 8626.71 ± 0.08) and 2026 (8626.39 ± 1.17 ± 0.80 MHz, agreement). No published re-measurement of the 18.5 GHz line surfaced; the group attributes earlier shifts to microwave reflections / asymmetric line shapes.

> You should know · The new "shortfall" organ reads each CKM unitarity cell independently, but several of its roads share the same inputs, so its 36 cells are not 36 independent tests.
>
> **The 36 CKM cells overlap heavily**
> * The new organ ΑΠΟΛΕΙΨΙΣ pairs six ways of measuring |V_ud| with six ways of measuring |V_us| and reads whether each pair adds up to 1. It counts **12 short, 5 over, 19 meeting**.
> * Those counts read like a tally of independent checks, but they are not:
> * The S and S′ rows are the **same measurement** with one error term dropped, so every S′ cell is just an S cell with a tighter width.

> * All 36 cells share the single |V_ub| value.
> * The organ's own notes say the cells are read independently and name the shared inputs as a blind spot, but the headline counts and the "12 short" figure will travel into the canon and registers without that caveat attached.

=== 2026-11-01T12:37:09.181Z USER (enqueue)
Here is a note offered by a side agent:

> Heads up · The torsion the main agent is now explaining is mostly an artifact of the integer basis the symbols were built on, not a property of the gravity states.
>
> **Most of the torsion comes from the coefficient choice**
> * The new ΓΟΜΟΣ organ builds each gravity state's Manin symbols over integers and reads where the module has **torsion** (extra finite pieces that vanish over the rationals), as a per-state signature.
> * The probe shows the torsion primes are almost the same for every state at a given weight: {2} at weight 4, {2,3} at weight 6, {2,3,5} at weight 8. Only a handful of states deviate.
> * That pattern is what the integer polynomial basis Sym^(k−2) itself produces: its coefficients are binomials, so primes up to k−2 appear for any group. A different integer basis (divided powers) would move or erase this part.
> * Only the exceptions (the extra 3 or 5 at low weight) and the **sizes** of the defects can carry information about a state.
> * **Before any page stakes "torsion = ramification", separate the basis-induced part from the state-dependent part**, otherwise the finding is a fact about the chosen basis.

 fix if true, then proceed. proceed otherwise /clear-thought

> You should know · The ASD page's own run already shows the bipyramid's denominator growth has an exact closed form, which is a theorem-shaped lemma, not a numerical surprise.
>
> **The bipyramid's denominators follow an exact formula**
> * The new page (ΑΣΥΜΦΩΝΙΑ) stakes that the bipyramid's form has "unbounded denominators" — the hallmark of a non-congruence group. The exact road just read them at five sample points: 72, 148, 297, 596, 897 at n = 50, 100, 200, 400, 600.
> * Those five numbers equal exactly **n + v₃(n!)** (how many factors of 3 are in n factorial), i.e. (3n − digit-sum-of-n-in-base-3)/2, every time.
> * That is what the cube root alone predicts: the binomial series for (1+x)^(−1/3) has exactly those powers of 3 in its denominators, and nothing in the rest of the construction cancels them because the series' leading coefficients are units at 3.
> * So "unbounded denominators" here is **not a measurement that needed 600 terms** — it is a two-line lemma, and the main agent is now writing it into the gate as an exact law (DENLAW-EXACT).
> * **Keep this in mind for the write-up:** the earning is the ASD congruences and the forced partner eigenvalue, not the denominator growth; if the denominator law is headlined, it will read like the bezel's cube law did — a known fact re-derived.
